**I have given to the son of your master.** The king is permitted to transfer an estate¹ as it is said (Shmuel I, 8:14) Within the jurisdiction of the monarchy is [the power] to take their fields and their vineyards and to give it to his servants.

footnotes:
¹ From its previous rightful owner to another person of his choice. This is Rebbe Yosi’s view in Sanhedrin 20b that those royal powers spoken of in Shmuel are to be taken literally as belonging to the king and not just a means of frightening the people into respecting the king which is Rebbe Yehudi’s view there in Sanhedrin, (See also Tosfos, ibid).



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Samuel 9:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/9:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/9:11:1)


**כֵּן יַעֲשֶׂה עַבְדֶּךָ וּמְפִיבֹשֶׁת אֹכֵל עַל שֻׁלְחָנִי.** צִיבָא אָמַר: **כֵּן יַעֲשֶׂה עַבְדֶּךָ**, וְדָוִד הֵשִׁיב: **וּמְפִיבֹשֶׁת אֹכֵל עַל שֻׁלְחָנִי**:


**So will your servant do.** Mephivoshes will eat at my table. Tsivoh said, "So will your servant do." And Dovid responded, "Mephivoshes will eat at my table."²

footnotes:
² The verse contains the words of two separate speakers, Tzivoh is speaking in the first part of the verse and Dovid responds in the second part.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Samuel 9:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/9:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/9:12:1)


**מוֹשַׁב בֵּית צִיבָא.** בָּנָיו וַעֲבָדָיו:


**Those who dwelled in the house of Tsivoh.** His children and his slaves.³

footnotes:
³ Mentioned in verse 10.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on II Samuel 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:2:1)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה אָבִיו עִמָּדִי חֶסֶד.** וּמַהוּ הַחֶסֶד, כְּשֶׁהָיָה דָוִד בּוֹרֵחַ מִפְּנֵי שָׁאוּל, בָּאוּ אֵלָיו אָבִיו וְאִמּוֹ וְאֶחָיו (שמואל א כב:ד): ״וַיַּנְחֵם אֶת פְּנֵי מֶלֶךְ מוֹאָב״, וַיֹּאמֶר לוֹ יֵשֶׁב נָא אָבִי וְאִמִּי עִמָּךְ, וַהֲרָגָם, חוּץ מֵאֶחָד מֵהֶם שֶׁבָּרַח וְנִמְלַט לְאֶרֶץ בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן אֵצֶל נָחָשׁ, (במדרש רבי תנחומא בוירא אליו):


**As his father acted with kindness towards me.** And what was the kindness? When Dovid was fleeing because of Shaul, his father, mother and brothers came to [join] him. "Dovid led them before the king of Moav"¹ [Dovid] said to him, "Please, [let] my father and mother dwell with you." [The king of Moav] killed them [all] except for one of them [i.e., the brothers] who fled and escaped to the land of the people of Amon, to Nochosh. This is a Midrash Rebbe Tanchuma in [Parshas] Vayeiroh ailov.

footnotes:
¹ Shmuel I, 22:4.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Samuel 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:3:1)


**הַמְכַבֵּד דָּוִד אֶת אָבִיךָ בְּעֵינֶיךָ.** הַנִּרְאֶה בְּעֵינֶיךָ שֶׁדָּוִד מְכַבֵּד אֶת אָבִיךָ, הֵם מֻזְהָרִים לֹא תִדְרֹשׁ שְׁלוֹמָם, וְהוּא יִדְרֹשׁ שְׁלוֹמְךָ:


**Do you think Dovid honors your father.** Does it appear in your eyes that Dovid is honoring your father? They are warned, "Do not seek peace with them",² and he is seeking peace with you?

footnotes:
² Devorim 23:7. This verse is referring to Amon and Moav mentioned earlier in verse 4.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Samuel 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:4:1)


**מַדְוֵיהֶם.** חֲלוּקֵיהֶם:


**Their clothes.** Their robes.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:4:2)


**שְׁתוּתֵיהֶם.** הָעֲגָבוֹת:


**Their buttocks.** The buttocks.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Samuel 10:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/10:16:1)


**וְשׁוֹבַךְ שַׂר צְבָא הֲדַדְעֶזֶר.** עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהָיָה גִבּוֹר וְעָרִיץ מְאֹד הִזְכִּירוֹ בִּשְׁמוֹ:


**Shovach the commander of Hadarezer's aray.** Because he was very mighty and powerful, he is mentioned by name.



## Chapter 11


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Samuel 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:1:1)


**לְעֵת צֵאת הַמַּלְאָכִים.** יֵשׁ עֵת בַּשָּׁנָה שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הַחַיָּלוֹת לָצֵאת, כְּשֶׁהָאָרֶץ מְלֵאָה קָמוֹת, וְהַסּוּסִים מוֹצְאִים תְּבוּאָה בַּשָּׂדֶה לֶאֱכוֹל:


**At the time [of year] when kings go forth.** There is a time of year when it is the practice that troops go forth, when the land is full of growth and the horses find produce in the field to eat.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Samuel 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:4:1)


**מִטֻּמְאָתָהּ.** מִנִּדָּתָהּ:


**From her uncleanliness.** From her menstrual impurity.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Samuel 11:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:6:1)


**שְׁלַח אֵלַי אֶת אוּרִיָּה.** שֶׁהָיָה מִתְכַּוֵּין שֶׁיִּשְׁכַּב עִם אִשְׁתּוֹ, וִיהֵא סָבוּר שֶׁמִּמֶּנּוּ הִיא מְעֻבֶּרֶת:


**Send me Urioh.** [Dovid] intended that he [Urioh] lie with his wife in order that it be thought that from him [Urioh] she was pregnant.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Samuel 11:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:8:1)


**מַשְׂאַת הַמֶּלֶךְ.** (תרגום:) ״סְעוּדְתָּא דְּמַלְכָּא״:


**The king's meal.** [Yonasan translates:] the king's meal.¹

footnotes:
¹ Dovid sent men with provisions for Urioh to be eaten at his home.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Samuel 11:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:15:1)


**וְנִכָּה וָמֵת.** כְּדֵי שֶׁתְּהֵא מְגֹרֶשֶׁת לְמַפְרֵעַ, וְנִמְצָא שֶׁלֹּא בָּא עַל אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ, שֶׁכָּל הַיּוֹצֵא לַמִּלְחָמָה, כּוֹתֵב גֵּט לְאִשְׁתּוֹ עַל תְּנַאי, אִם יָמוּת בַּמִּלְחָמָה (שבת נו א):


**And he will be struck and killed.** In order for [Batsheva] to be divorced [from Urioh] retroactively. the result would be that [Dovid] did not cohabit with a married woman. Because all [soldiers] who went out to war wrote a conditional divorce document for their wife should they die in battle.²

footnotes:
² Rashi in Kesubos 9b gives this same explanation. Tosafos there explains that Rashi means that the condition was not necessarily that the soldier dies, but even if he does not return at the end of the war, the divorce would be valid.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on II Samuel 11:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:21:1)


**בֶּן יְרֻבֶּשֶׁת.** הוּא גִּדְעוֹן, שֶׁעָשָׂה מְרִיבָה עִם הַבֹּשֶׁת, הוּא הַבַּעַל:


**The son of Yerubeshes.** This is Gidon who fought with Boshes, which is the Baal.³

footnotes:
³ See Shoftim 6:32. The name יְרֻבֶּשֶׁת is a contraction of the two words, יָרֶב and בָשֶת, fighting with Boshes. Avimelech’s death, referred to here, is related in Shoftim 9:53.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on II Samuel 11:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:25:1)


**הַחֲזֵק מִלְחַמְתְּךָ אֶל הָעִיר וְהָרְסָהּ.** עַד כָּאן דִּבְרֵי הַשְּׁלִיחוּת:


**Intensify your war [against] the city and destroy it.** The words of the message⁴ [end] at this point.

footnotes:
⁴ From Dovid to Yoav.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 11:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/11:25:2)


**וְחַזְּקֵהוּ.** דָּוִד אָמַר לַמַּלְאָךְ, וְחַזְּקֵהוּ לְיוֹאָב בְּדִבְרֵי תַנְחוּמִין, שֶׁלֹּא יֵרַךְ לִבּוֹ:


**You should encourage him [Yoav].** Dovid [then] said to the messenger, "Encourage Yoav with consoling words so that his heart should not weaken.



## Chapter 12


### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:4:1)


**וַיָּבֹא הֵלֶךְ.** דִּמָּה אֶת יֵצֶר הָרָע תְּחִלָּה לְהֵלֶךְ, שֶׁעוֹבֵר לְדַרְכּוֹ, וְאַחַר כָּךְ לְאוֹרֵחַ, שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה אַכְסְנַאי, וְאַחַר כָּךְ לְאִישׁ, שֶׁהוּא בַּעַל הַבַּיִת:


**A traveler came.** [Noson] compared the evil inclination at first to a sojourner that is [quickly] going on his way. Afterwords he is compared to a guest that has become a resident and afterwords he is compared to a man who is the owner of the house.¹

footnotes:
¹ This verse is Noson’s parable alluding to the evil inclination. The visiton is first referred to as הֵלֶךְ, then אׂרֵחַ and finally as an אִישׁ, representing the evil inclination’s ability to take more and more control over its victim’s life.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:5:1)


**כִּי בֶן מָוֶת.** הַגּוֹזֵל אֶת הֶעָנִי, כְּאִלּוּ נוֹטֵל אֶת נַפְשׁוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (משלי א:יט): ״אֶת נֶפֶשׁ בְּעָלָיו יִקָּח״:


**Is worthy of death.** Someone who steals from a poor person is tantamount to taking his life² as it is said, "He has taken it's owner's life."³

footnotes:
² See Baba Kama 119a. Stealing a pruta from anyone is tantamount to killing him.
³ Mishlei 1:19.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:6:1)


**יְשַׁלֵּם אַרְבַּעְתָּיִם.** כָּךְ אֵרַע לוֹ, שֶׁלָּקָה בְּאַרְבָּעָה בָּנִים, הַיֶּלֶד וְאַמְנוֹן תָּמָר וְאַבְשָׁלוֹם:


**Pay fourfold.** This is what [eventually] happened, he was punished with [problems concerning] four children the child⁴ and Amnon Tomor and Avsholom.⁵

footnotes:
⁴ Related further on in verse 15.
⁵ The events with Amnon, Tomor and Avsholom start in chapter 13.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:8:1)


**וְאֶת נְשֵׁי אֲדֹנֶיךָ.** מִיכַל בַּת שָׁאוּל:


**Your master's woman.** Michal, the daughter of Shaul.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:8:2)


**וְאֹסִפָה.** וְהָיִיתִי מוֹסִיף לְךָ:


**I will add on.** I would add on for you.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:14:1)


**כִּי נִאֵץ נִאַצְתָּ אֶת אוֹיְבֵי.** כִּנּוּי הוּא זֶה, דֶּרֶךְ כָּבוֹד לְמַעְלָה וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״אֲרֵי מֵיפְתַּח פְּתַחְתָּא פּוּמָא דְּסָנְאֵי עַמָּא דַה׳״:


**Because you have angered the enemies of Adonoy.** This is a euphimism a way [to be] respectable to on High.⁶ Yonasan translated, Because you have opened up you have opened the mouth of [those] who hate the people of God.

footnotes:
⁶ Using “enemies of God” to stand in for God in order for the word “angered” not to have to be applied to Him in a direct way.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:15:1)


**וַיֵּאָנַשׁ.** לְשׁוֹן חֹלִי:


**[He became sick.]** [This word] denotes sickness.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:16:1)


**וּבָא וְלָן וְשָׁכַב אָרְצָה.** וּבָא הַבַּית, וְלָן בַּלַּיְלָה שׁוֹכֵב לָאָרֶץ:


**And came to repose and lied on the ground.** And came [back] to the house and reposed at night lying on the ground.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:17:1)


**וְלֹא בָרָה.** לְשׁוֹן אֲכִילָה, כְּמוֹ (שמואל ב יג:ו): ״וְאֶבְרֶה מִיָּדָהּ״:


**And he did not eat.** [A word] denoting eating like [we find], "I will eat from her hand."⁷

footnotes:
⁷ Later, 13:6.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:25:1)


**בַּעֲבוּר ה׳.** אֲשֶׁר אֲהֵבוֹ:


**Because of God.** That loved him [Shlomo].⁸

footnotes:
⁸ יֽדִידְיָה is a concatanation of יֽדִיד, beloved one and יָה, God.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:26:1)


**אֶת עִיר הַמְּלוּכָה.** שְׁנֵי חִזּוּקֵי חוֹמָה הָיוּ בָּהּ, חִיצוֹנָה וּפְנִימִית, עִיר הַחִיצוֹנָה, עִיר מְלוּכָה, וְהַפְּנִימִית, לְמִבְצָר וּלְחוֹזֶק:


**The royal city.** There were two enforced walls [of cities] in it, an outside one and an inside one. The outside city was [called] the royal city, and the inside [city] was a fortress and stronghold.⁹

footnotes:
⁹ Yoav left this one for Dovid to conquer as is stated in verse 29.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:30:1)


**אֶת עֲטֶרֶת מַלְכָּם.** תּוֹעֲבַת בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן ׳מִלְכֹּם׳ שְׁמוֹ, לְשׁוֹן מֹלֶךְ:


**The crown of Malkom.** The abomination [idol] of the people of Ammon's name was Malkom. Denoting [the word] Moleich.¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ מוֹלֶךְ is an idol to whom children were sacrificed.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:30:2)


**וַתְּהִי עַל רֹאשׁ דָּוִד.** אִתַּי הַגִּתִּי בִּטְּלָהּ (עבודה זרה מד א):


**It was [hung over] Dovid's head.** Itai the Gitite annulled it.¹¹

footnotes:
¹¹ This explanation of the verse is stated in Avodah Zoroh 24. It is prohibited for Jews to derive any pleasure from the idols of gentiles. They must be anulled by a gentile, i.e., taken out of the service of idol worship, after which they are permitted to Jews. Itai the Gitite, a gentile, did this to Malkom for Dovid’s sake.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:31:1)


**מְגֵרָה וַחֲרִצִים וּמַגְזְרוֹת.** כְּמִין פְּצִירָה פִּים שֶׁקּוֹרִין לימ״א, מִינֵי יִסּוּרִים הֵם:


**Saw-like utensils, iron threshing utensils and iron axes.** They are types of tortures.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:31:2)


**מְגֵרָה.** סַכִּין פָּגוּם פְּגִימוֹת הַרְבֵּה תְּכוּפוֹת זוֹ לָזוֹ:


**Saw-like utensils.** A knife serrated with many [sharp] edges bent towards each other.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:31:3)


**חֲרִצִים.** הוּא מוֹרַג חָרוּץ מָלֵא חֲרִיצִים, כְּמִין פְּצִירָה פִּים שֶׁקּוֹרִין לימ״א:


**Threshing utensils.** This is a hacked saw full of cuts like "many sharp edges"¹² that is called laime (O.F.).

footnotes:
¹² Shmuel I, 13:21.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 12:31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/12:31:4)


**בַּמַּלְבֵּן.** בְּטִיט חוּצוֹת, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וּגְרַר יָתְהוֹן בְּשׁוּקַיָּא״:


**The streets.** In the mud of the streets and so Yonason [also] translated: "He dragged them in the streets."



## Chapter 13


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:1:1)


**וּלְאַבְשָׁלוֹם אָחוֹת יָפָה.** שֶׁהָיְתָה בַּת אִמּוֹ:


**Avsholom had a pretty sister.** That was the daughter of his mother.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:2:1)


**לְהִתְחַלּוֹת.** עַד שֶׁנֶּחֱלָה:


**To [the point of] sickness.** Until he became sick.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:2:2)


**כִּי בְתוּלָה הִיא.** צְנוּעָה בַּבַּיִת וְאֵינָהּ יוֹצֵאת לַחוּץ, לְפִיכָךְ **וַיִּפָּלֵא בְּעֵינֵי אַמְנוֹן וְגוֹ׳,** נִכְסֶה וְנֶעְלָם מִמֶּנּוּ מָה תּוֹאֲנָה יָכוֹל לְבַקֵּשׁ וְיִשְׁכַּב עִמָּהּ, כְּמוֹ (דברים יז:ח): ,כִּי יִפָּלֵא מִמְּךָ״, ״אֲרֵי יִתְכַּסֵּי מִמָּךְ״:


**Because she was a virgin.** She was modest [and stayed] in the house and did not go outside therefore, **"It was hidden from the eyes of Amnon…** It was covered and hidden from him. What excuse he could seek [in order] to lay with her.¹ Similar to "When it is concealed from you",² [which] Onkelus translates: "When it is hidden from you."

footnotes:
¹ According to Rashi, the words in our verse, “because she was a virgin” do not tell us why Amnon was desirous of her. The reason for his desire was because she was pretty. Rashi consequently explains that these words are telling us why she was inaccessible to Amnon and are connected to the words that follow, “it was hidden in the eyes of Amnon” to find a way to lay with her.
² Devorim 17:8.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:3:1)


**אִישׁ חָכָם.** לְרִשְׁעָה:


**Smart man.** For wickedness.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:4:1)


**דַּל.** כָּחוּשׁ, כְּמוֹ (בראשית מא:יט): ״דַּלּוֹת וְרָעוֹת תֹּאַר״:


**Weak.** Weak as [we find] "weak and bad [in] appearance".³

footnotes:
³ Bereishis 41:19.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:5:1)


**אֶת הַבִּרְיָה.** אֶת הַסְּעוּדָה:


**The meal.** The meal.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:8:1)


**וַתְּלַבֵּב.** וַתַּחֲלוֹט, סֹלֶת מֻרְבֶּכֶת בְּמַיִם רוֹתְחִין תְּחִלָּה, וְאַחַר כָּךְ בְּשֶׁמֶן:


**She made a paste.** She made a paste of flour mixed [it] first into boiling water and afterwords in oil.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:9:1)


**אֶת הַמַּשְׂרֵת.** תַּרְגּוּם שֶׁל מַחֲבַת, מַסְרֵיתָא:


**The pan.** The [Onkelus'] translation of Machues is "pan".⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Vayikro 6:14.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:13:1)


**כִּי לֹא יִמְנָעֵנִי מִמֶּךָּ.** שֶׁמֻּתֶּרֶת אֲנִי לְךָ, לְפִי שֶׁנִּתְעַבְּרָה בִּי אִמִּי כְּשֶׁהִיא נָכְרִית יְפַת תֹּאַר, שֶׁלְּקָחָהּ דָּוִד בַּמִּלְחָמָה, וּמִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ בֵּן אוֹ בַּת מִן שִׁפְחָה, אֵינוֹ בְּנוֹ לְכָל דָּבָר:


**Because he will not keep me from you.** I am permitted to you because my mother became pregnant with me when she was still a gentile, pretty woman⁵ that Dovid took conjugally in war. And someone who has a son or daughter from a gentile handmaid is not considered his child for any legal matter.

footnotes:
⁵ The laws concerning the taking of gentile woman during war is discused in Devorim begginning in 21:10. The Torah calls her a יְפַת תּוֹאַר, “pretty woman.”



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:15:1)


**וַיִּשְׂנָאֶהָ אַמְנוֹן.** אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (סנהדרין כא א): נִימָא נִקְשְׁרָה לוֹ, וַעֲשָׂאַתוּ כְּרוּת שָׁפְכָה:


**Amnon hated her.** Our Rabbis say she tied a hair for him [in his genital area] and caused him to have a severed organ.⁶

footnotes:
⁶ One who is so injured is prohibited from marrying a Jewish woman as related in Devorim 23:2. This interpretation of our verse is in Sanhedrin 21a.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:16:1)


**אַל אוֹדוֹת.** אַל תַּעֲשֶׂה אוֹדוֹת הָרָעָה הַזֹּאת שֶׁל שִׁלּוּחַ, הַגְּדוֹלָה מִן הָאַחֶרֶת שֶׁעָשִׂיתָ עִמִּי וְעִנִּיתָנִי, וְעוֹד תּוֹסִיף רָעָה מִמֶּנָּה לְשַׁלְּחֵנִי:


**Do not do this thing.** Do not do this evil thing of sending [me] away which is worse then the other [evil thing] that you did to me. You raped me and then you added on more evil than that by sending me away.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:23:1)


**וַיִּהְיוּ גוֹזְזִים.** וְדַרְכָּם הָיָה לַעֲשׂוֹת מִשְׁתֶּה בִּגְזוֹז צֹאנָם:


**The were shearing.** It was there custom to make a party when they sheared their flocks.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:26:1)


**וְלֹא יֵלֶךְ נָא אִתָּנוּ.** וְאִם לֹא יָכוֹל אַתָּה לָלֶכֶת, יֵלֶךְ נָא עִמָּנוּ אַמְנוֹן:


**Will not come with us.** If you can not go, let Amnon go with us.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:32:1)


**עַל פִּי אַבְשָׁלוֹם הָיְתָה שׂוּמָה.** בְּצִוּוּיוֹ שֶׁל אַבְשָׁלוֹם הָיְתָה שִׂימָה זוֹ נִשֹּׁמֶת עַל עֲבָדָיו, לַהֲרוֹג אֶת אַמְנוֹן:


**This has been Avsholom's order.** By Avsholom's command was this order bestowed on his servants to kill Amnon.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:39:1)


**וַתְּכַל דָּוִד.** הֲרֵי זֶה מִקְרָא קָצָר, וַתְּכַל נֶפֶשׁ דָּוִד; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְחֲמִידַת נַפְשָׁא דְדָוִד״, כְּמוֹ (תהלים פד:ג): ״נִכְסְפָה וְגַם כָּלְתָה״, לְשׁוֹן תַּאֲוָה:


**Dovid desired.** This is a shortened verse, [it means to say:] "Dovid's soul desired." Yonoson also translates [this way:] "Dovid's soul desired," as [we find:] "[My Soul] yearns, and even pines."⁷ Language [denoting] desire.

footnotes:
⁷ Tehilim 84:3.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 13:39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/13:39:2)


**כִּי נִחַם עַל אַמְנוֹן.** קִבֵּל תַּנְחוּמִין:


**Because he was consoled concerning Amnon.** He accepted [words of] consolation.



## Chapter 14


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:2:1)


**וַיִּשְׁלַח יוֹאָב תְּקוֹעָה.** אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (מנחות פה ב): כִּי שֶׁמֶן זַיִת מָצוּי שָׁם, לְכָךְ חָכְמָה מְצוּיָה שָׁם:


**Yoav sent to Tekuah.** Our Rabbis said Because olive oil is found there, therefore wisdom is found there.¹

footnotes:
¹ Menochos 85a.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:5:1)


**אֲבָל אִשָּׁה אַלְמָנָה אָנִי.** אֲבָל, ״בְּקוּשְׁטָא״:


**In truth, I am a widowed woman.**אֲבָל means "In truth."



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:9:1)


**עָלַי אֲדֹנִי הַמֶּלֶךְ הֶעָוֹן.** כִּנּוּי הוּא לִכְבוֹדוֹ, כְּלוֹמַר אַתָּה מַדְחֶה אוֹתִי לֵאמֹר אֲנִי מְצַוֶּה עָלֶיךָ וַאֲנִי אֵלֵךְ לְדַרְכִּי, וְאַתָּה לֹא תְצַוֶּה עָלַי, וּבְנִי יֵהָרֵג, עַל מִי הֶעָוֹן הַזֶּה:


**My master the king, the sin is upon me.** This is euphemistic for [the sake of] his honour in other words, "You are rebutting me [by] saying, 'I will command [someone] concerning you,' I will [then] go on my way and you will not command [anyone] concerning me, and my son will get murdered. Upon who is this sin?"



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:11:1)


**יִזְכָּר נָא הַמֶּלֶךְ אֶת ה׳.** שֶׁהִקְפִּיד עַל אֹרֶךְ הַדֶּרֶךְ לְהַצִּיל נְפָשׁוֹת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (דברים יט:ו): ״וְהִשִּׂיגוֹ כִּי יִרְבֶּה הַדֶּרֶךְ״, וְזֶהוּ מֵהַרְבּוֹת גֹּאֵל הַדָּם לְשַׁחֵת, וְאַתָּה דוֹחֶה אוֹתִי:


**Please remember Adonoy.** God was stringent concerning the distance [necessary] to save lives² as it is said, "He will overtake him because the way was long".³ This is [her reference with], "To avoid giving more [time] for the avenger to destroy," and you are pushing me [away].

footnotes:
² When a person kills accidently, he must go to a city of refuge. These cities should never be too distant from any potential fugitive who may need to go there.
³ Devorim 19:5.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:13:1)


**וְלָמָּה חָשַׁבְתָּה כָזֹאת.** שֶׁחָשַׁדְתָּ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ לַהֲרוֹג אֶת הַשֵּׁנִי בְּלֹא עֵדִים וְהַתְרָאָה:


**Why did you think this.** You suspected that a Jew could come to kill another [Jew] without witnesses and a warning.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:13:2)


**וּמִדַּבֵּר הַמֶּלֶךְ הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה כְּאָשֵׁם.** וְהַדָּבָר הַזֶּה שֶׁחָרַצְתָּ לַאֲחֵרִים, שֶׁאָמַרְתָּ לִי לֹא יִפּוֹל מִשַּׂעֲרַת בְּנֵךְ אַרְצָה, עַכְשָׁיו שֶׁאֲגַלֶּה לְךָ שֶׁאֵין הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה אֶלָּא עַל שְׁנֵי בָּנֶיךָ, אַל תִּתְחָרֵט לֵאמֹר דִּבַּרְתִּי זֶה כְּאָשֵׁם, וְהִנְנִי חוֹזֵר בִּי, לְבִלְתִּי הָשִׁיב הַמֶּלֶךְ אֶת אַבְשָׁלוֹם בְּנוֹ שֶׁנִּדְחָה מִמֶּנּוּ וּבָרַח לוֹ:


**That which the king said he himself is guilty of.** This thing that you have decided for others, that you said to me "hair from your son shall not fall to the ground." Now that I have revealed to you that this thing never happened, but it was only [a parable] to your two sons, do not reverse yourself saying, "I said this mistakingly and I have reconsidered" in order that the king not bring back his son, Avsholom that his been exiled from him and has fled.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:13:3)


**וּמִדַּבֵּר הַמֶּלֶךְ.** אוקינו״ן פרלטי״ש בלע״ז:


**The king will say.** *Aucenon parleter* in O.F.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:14:1)


**כִּי מוֹת נָמוּת.** וְדַיֵּינוּ בְּאוֹתוֹ עֹנֶשׁ:


**We are [all] going to die.** And that punishment is sufficent.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ For Avsholom.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:14:2)


**וְלֹא יִשָּׂא אֱלֹהִים נֶפֶשׁ.** אִישׁ מִן הַמִּיתָה, לְפִיכָךְ וְחָשַׁב הַמֶּלֶךְ מַחֲשָׁבוֹת לְבִלְתִּי יִדַּח מִמֶּנּוּ נִדָּח:


**God will not countenance anyone.** a man from [avoiding] death. Therefore the king should devise means to avoid having his exiled one remain an outcast.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:15:1)


**וְעַתָּה אֲשֶׁר בָּאתִי לְדַבֵּר אֶל הַמֶּלֶךְ אֲדֹנִי אֶת הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה.** בְּכִנּוּי עָלַי וְעַל בְּנִי:


**And now I came to speak to my master, the king these words.** As a parable about myself and my son


###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:15:2)


**כִּי יֵרְאוּנִי הָעָם.** הִפְחִידוּנִי מִלְּבַקֵּשׁ אֶת אֲדֹנִי עַל בְּנוֹ, פֶּן יִכְעוֹס עָלַי:


**Because the people frightened me.** They frightened me [away] from imploring my master concerning [the plight of] his son lest he become angry with me.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:15:3)


**וַתֹּאמֶר שִׁפְחָתְךָ אֲדַבְּרָה נָּא.** כְּעִנְיָן זֶה, **אוּלַי יַעֲשֶׂה וְגוֹ׳**:


**Your handmaiden said, "I will speak."** In this way [with a parable], perhaps he will take action etc.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:16:1)


**כִּי יִשְׁמַע הַמֶּלֶךְ לְהַצִּיל אֶת אֲמָתוֹ מִכַּף הָאִישׁ.** הַבָּא לַהֲרוֹג אֶת בְּנִי, וּלְהַשְׁמִידֵנוּ יַחַד מִנַּחֲלַת אֱלֹהִים:


**When the king hears** [and agrees] to save his handmaiden from the [avenger] man's hand. who is coming to kill my son and to remove us both from the inheritance [given by] God.⁵

footnotes:
⁵ All this is what the woman was conveying in her parable. This was all designed to be tolerable for the king to hear.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:17:1)


**וַתֹּאמֶר שִׁפְחָתְךָ.** מֵאַחַר שֶׁיְּצַוֶּה הַמֶּלֶךְ עָלַי, יִהְיֶה דְּבָרוֹ לִמְנוּחָה לִבְנִי, כִּי לֹא יָשׁוּב מִדְּבָרוֹ הַטּוֹב, כִּי כְּמַלְאַךְ ה׳ אֲדֹנִי הַמֶּלֶךְ, וְלֹא יַחֲזִירֶנּוּ כַּעַס וְשִׂנְאָה מִדְּבָרוֹ הַטּוֹב:


**Your handmaiden says.** Now that the king has ordered [remedial action] concerning me, his word should provide relief for my son because he will not back away from his kind decision⁶ because my master, the king is like an angel of God and anger and hatred will not change his kind decision.

footnotes:
⁶ You came to the kind decision to provide relief to my make believe son in the parable. Please don’t change that decision when deliberating about Avsholom who in real-life needs relief.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:19:1)


**אִם אִשׁ.** כְּמוֹ אִם יֵשׁ, כְּמוֹ הֲיֵשׁ, וְכֵן (מיכה ו:י): ״עוֹד הָאִשׁ בֵּית רָשָׁע״, כְּמוֹ הֲיֵשׁ:


**It is impossible.** [This is] like "if it is possible" or like "is it possible" So too [we find] "Is there in the house of a wicked person"⁷ meaning "is there".⁸

footnotes:
⁷ Micha 6:10.
⁸ הָאִש is standing in for הֲיֵשׁ which means “is there”.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:20:1)


**לְבַעֲבוּר סַבֵּב.** לְגַלְגֵּל, עַד שֶׁיֵּצֵא דְּבַר בְּנוֹ שֶׁל הַמֶּלֶךְ לָאוֹר:


**In order to skirt around.** To skirt around [the issue] until the matter concerning the king's son comes to light.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:20:2)


**וַאדֹנִי חָכָם.** וְהֵבַנְתָּ כִּי מֵאֵת יוֹאָב יָצָאתָ:


**[But] my master is wise.** and you understood that this is coming from Yoav.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:30:1)


**אֶל יָדִי.** סָמוּךְ לִרְשׁוּתִי, בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֲנִי יָכוֹל לְהַזִּיק:


**Which is near me.** Near my domain in a place where I can inflict damage.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:30:2)


**אֶל יָדִי.** אינמי״ש אוש״ט בלע״ז:


**Which is near me.** En mains aussi in O.F.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on II Samuel 14:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/14:32:1)


**וֶהֱמִתָנִי.** וְיַהֲרֹג אוֹתִי הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**[Then] kill me.** The king should kill me.



## Chapter 15


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:1:1)


**וַחֲמִשִּׁים אִישׁ וגו׳.** כֻּלָּם נְטוּלֵי טְחוֹל, וַחֲקוּקֵי כַּפּוֹת רַגְלַיִם:


**And [an enfourage of] fifty men etc.** All of them had their thighs removed and the heals of their feet cut out.¹

footnotes:
¹ Sanhedrin 21a. Rashi there explains that not having thighs enabled them to run faster and not having heels enabled them to step on sharp objects without any sensation of pain.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:2:1)


**וְהִשְׁכִּים אַבְשָׁלוֹם.** בְּכָל בֹּקֶר:


**Avsholom rose early.** Every morning.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:2:2)


**מֵאַחַד שִׁבְטֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עַבְדֶּךָ.** מִשֵּׁבֶט פְּלוֹנִי:


**From one of the tribes of Yisroel.** From so and so tribe.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:4:1)


**וְהִצְדַּקְתִּיו.** (תרגום:) ״וַאֲדִינְנֵיהּ בְּקוּשְׁטָא״:


**And I will judge him righteously.** [Targum Yonosan translates:] "And I will judge him truthfully."



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:7:1)


**מִקֵּץ אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה.** שֶׁשָּׁאֲלוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל מֶלֶךְ מִשְּׁמוּאֵל, נִתְגַּלְגֵּל דְּבַר מֶרֶד וְהַשְׁפָּלָה בַּמַּלְכוּת:


**At the end of forty years.** [After] Yisroel had asked Shmuel for a king this rebellion and weakening of the kingdom occurred.²

footnotes:
² This is the opinion of Rebe Nehuroi in the name of Rebbe Yohoshua brought in Temurah 14b. Rashi is explaining that these forty years are not describing how long Avsholom was engaged in his intriguing against the king. Rather, the verse is informing us that forty years after their improper request, the kingdom was weakened.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:7:2)


**אֵלְכָה נָּא.** לְחֶבְרוֹן:


**I would like to go.** To Chevron.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:7:3)


**וַאֲשַׁלֵּם אֶת נִדְרִי.** אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (סוטה לד ב): לְהָבִיא כְּבָשִׂים מֵחֶבְרוֹן, כִּי שָׁם הָיוּ כְּבָשִׂים שְׁמֵנִים:


**And fulfill the vows [of bringing sacrifices]** Our Rabbis said, "[Avsholom wanted] to bring lambs from Chevron³ because there, the lambs were fat."⁴

footnotes:
³ Ibid.
⁴ Rashi there brings the Talmud in Menachos 87a that discusses the quality of the lambs of Chevron.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:11:1)


**קְרֻאִים וְהֹלְכִים לְתֻמָּם.** מְפֹרָשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת סוֹטָה יְרוּשַׁלְמִית (א ח) שֶׁבִּקֵּשׁ מֵאָבִיו שֶׁיִּכְתֹּב לוֹ, שֶׁכָּל שְׁנֵי בְּנֵי אָדָם שֶׁיְּבַקֵּשׁ שֶׁיֵּלְכוּ עִמּוֹ, יֵלְכוּ וְהָיָה מַרְאֶה אוֹתוֹ לִשְׁנַיִם כָּאן, וְאַחַר כָּךְ לִשְׁנַיִם אֲחֵרִים, וְכֵן הַרְבֵּה:


**Invited and leaving in innovence.** It is explained in the Yerushalmi tractate of Sotah⁵ that he [Avsholom] requested of his father that he [Dovid] write [a document] for him that any two people that he request go with him, should go. He [proceeded] to show it [the document] to two people here and afterwords to two other people and in this way to many.⁶

footnotes:
⁵ Chapter 1, halach 8.
⁶ But he implied to Dovid that only two men would go. He kept using the same document for more and more pairs of men.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:17:1)


**בֵּית הַמֶּרְחָק.** (תרגום:) ״בַּאֲתַר דְּרָחִיק״:


**Far away.** [Targum Yonasan translates:] "in a far away place."



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:18:1)


**עַל יָדוֹ.** אֶצְלוֹ, הוּא עוֹמֵד וְהֵם עוֹבְרִים:


**By him.** Near him, He stood in place and they passed by.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:19:1)


**עִם הַמֶּלֶךְ.** עִם אַבְשָׁלוֹם:


**With the king.** With Avsholom.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:19:2)


**כִּי נָכְרִי אַתָּה.** וַאֲנַחְנוּ בּוֹרְחִים מֵאֵין צֵידָה וּמָזוֹן, וּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁאַתָּה נָכְרִי לֹא תִּמְצָא מְרַחֲמִים:


**Because you are a foreigner.** We are fleeing without provisions and food, and because you are a foreigner you will not find people that will have pity on you.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:19:3)


**וְגַם גּוֹלֶה אַתָּה לִמְקוֹמֶךָ.** וְגַם אִם גּוֹלֶה אַתָּה מֵעִם הַמֶּלֶךְ שֶׁאֵינְךָ רוֹצֶה לִהְיוֹת עִמּוֹ, לִמְקוֹמְךָ חֲזוֹר לְךָ, כִּי לֹא טוֹב לֶכְתְּךָ עִמִּי, שֶׁהֲרֵי תְּמוֹל בָּאתָ:


**And even if you are estranged [from Avsholom].** And even if you are exiled from the king [Avsholom] because you do not want to be with him, then return to your home because it is not good to go with me because you [just] came yesterday.⁷

footnotes:
⁷ This verse blends into verse 20 where Dovid explains more about why it is disadvantageous for Itai to stay with him.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:20:1)


**וְהַיּוֹם אֲנִיעֲךָ עִמָּנוּ.** וְאֵין לִי מָקוֹם שֶׁאוּכַל לְהוֹשִׁיבְךָ שָׁם וּלְהִמָּלֵט, כִּי אֲנִי הוֹלֵךְ אֶל אֲשֶׁר יִקְרֵנִי הַמִּקְרֶה לָלֶכֶת, יוֹם יוֹם כְּשֶׁאֶשְׁמַע שֶׁהָרוֹדְפִים כָּאן, אֲנִי בּוֹרֵחַ כָּאן, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״וְגַם גּוֹלֶה אַתָּה לִמְקוֹמֶךָ״ (לעיל פסוק יט): ״וְאַף אִם גְּלֵי אַתְּ אָזֵיל לָךְ לְאַתְרָךְ״:


**And should I today cause you to move with us.** I have no place that I can settle you there to escape because I am going to where circumstances have me go each day. When I hear that the pursuers are here I flee here. And so Yonasan translates [the verse], "And if you are estranged [from Avsholom] you can go home" [as:] "And if you you are estranged go [back] to your city."


###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:20:2)


**חֶסֶד וֶאֱמֶת.** אֲנִי מַחֲזִיק בְּךָ טוֹבַת חֶסֶד וֶאֱמֶת שֶׁעָשִׂיתָ עִמָּדִי:


**I will consider it kindness and truth.** I will credit you the favor of kindness and truth that you have done for me.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:22:1)


**לֵךְ וַעֲבֹר.** עִם שְׁאָר הָעָם, אַחַר אֲשֶׁר אֵינְךָ רוֹצֶה לְהִפָּרֵד מִמֶּנִּי:


**"Go and pass"** Together with the rest of the people now that you do not want to part from me.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:23:1)


**וְכָל הָעָם עֹבְרִים.** מִן הַמֶּלֶךְ וָהָלְאָה, לְפִי שֶׁהוּא עוֹמֵד, קוֹרֵא אוֹתָם עוֹבְרִים:


**And all the people were passing.** From the king and onwards according to his location they are called passers by.⁸

footnotes:
⁸ Even though he was also passing by, since they were ahead of him, they are called passers by relative to his location.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:24:1)


**וַיַּצִּקוּ.** כְּמוֹ וַיַּצִּיגוּ, (תרגום:) ״וַאֲקִימוּ יָת אֲרוֹנָא״, הֶעֱמִידוּהוּ לְצַד אֶחָד, וְהָעָם עוֹבְרִים, שֶׁהָיָה דָוִד רוֹצֶה לְשָׂאתוֹ עִמּוֹ:


**They set down.** Like, "They stopped,"⁹ [Yonasan translates:] "They stopped the Ark." They stood it off to one side and the people passed by because Dovid wanted it brought with him.

footnotes:
⁹ Previously in 6:17.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:24:2)


**וַיַּעַל אֶבְיָתָר עַד תֹּם וגו׳.** הֲרֵי זֶה מִקְרָא מְסֹרָס, וַיַּצִּיקוּ אֶת אֲרוֹן הָאֱלֹהִים עַד תֹּם כָּל הָעָם לַעֲבוֹר:


**And Evyosor went off [to the side]** until [all the people] finished etc. This is a shortened verse they set down the Ark of God until all the people finished travelling.¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ The words ויעל אביתר need to be placed at the end of the verse for interpretation.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:24:3)


**וַיַּעַל אֶבְיָתָר.** אוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם נִסְתַּלֵּק מִן הַכְּהוּנָּה, שֶׁשָּׁאַל בָּאוּרִים וְתוּמִּים וְלֹא נַעֲנָה, וְנִכְנַס צָדוֹק תַּחְתָּיו, וְכֵן שָׁנִינוּ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם וּבְסֵדֶר יוֹמָא (עג ב):


**Evyosor went off [to the side].** That day he was removed from the Kohanite service.¹¹ because He inquired of the Urim and Tumim but was not answered,¹² Tzodok then replaced him. This is what we learned in Seder Olam in tractate Yuma.¹³

footnotes:
¹¹ As the Kohen Gadol.
¹² This showed that he was no longer qualified for this lofty position. The Urim and Tumim lit up certain letters on the kohen Gadol’s breastplate as a means for God to communicate to His people. See Shemos 28:30.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:27:1)


**הֲרוֹאֶה אַתָּה.** אִם רוֹאֶה אַתָּה שֶׁעֵצָה נְכוֹנָה הִיא, שׁוּב הָעִיר וגו׳:


**If you see this [as right].** I you see this as a good idea, "return to the city etc.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:27:2)


**שְׁנֵי בְנֵיכֶם אִתְּכֶם.** בְּיָדָם תּוּכְלוּ לְהוֹדִיעֵנִי מַה שֶּׁתִּשְׁמְעוּ מִבֵּית הַמֶּלֶךְ, לְפִי מַה שֶּׁתּוֹדִיעוּנִי אִמָּלֵט:


**Your two sons with you.** It will be in their power to inform me what they hear in the king's palace. And based on what they inform me I can escape.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:30:1)


**בְּמַעֲלֵה הַזֵּיתִים.** בְּהַר הַזֵּיתִים:


**The heights of Mount Olives.** On Mount Olives.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:30:2)


**חָפוּי.** מְכֹרָךְ, כְּדֶרֶךְ הָאֲבֵלִים:


**Was covered.** "Covered" in the manner of mourners.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:30:3)


**חָפוּ.** (תרגום:) ״כְּרִיכוּ״:


**Covered.** [Yonasan translates:] "Covered."



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:31:1)


**וְדָוִד הִגִּיד.** וּלְדָוִד הִגִּיד הַמַּגִּיד:


**Dovid was told [by an informer].** Dovid was told by the informer.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:31:2)


**סַכֵּל.** קַלְקֵל, כְּמוֹ (קהלת י:יד): ״הַסָּכָל יַרְבֶּה דְבָרִים״:


**Ruin.** Ruin as we find "The tool talks too much".¹⁴

footnotes:
¹⁴ Koheles 10:14.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:32:1)


**בָּא עַד הָרֹאשׁ.** (תרגום:) ״עַד רֵישׁ טוּרְיָא״:


**Arrived at the summit.** At the mountain's summit.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:32:2)


**אֲשֶׁר יִשְׁתַּחֲוֶה שָׁם לֵאלֹהִים.** אֲשֶׁר הָיָה רָגִיל לְהִשְׁתַּחֲווֹת שָׁם כְּשֶׁהָיָה בָּא לִירוּשָׁלַיִם, הָיָה רוֹאֶה מִשָּׁם אֶת הָאֹהֶל שֶׁהָאָרוֹן בְּתוֹכוֹ, וְהָיָה מִשְׁתַּחֲוֶה:


**Where he used to bow down to Elokim.** Where he used to bow down. When he used to come to Yerusholoyim he would see from that [vantage] point the tent in which the Ark was [placed] and he would bow down.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:34:1)


**עֶבֶד אָבִיךָ וַאֲנִי מֵאָז.** כְּמוֹ וַאֲנִי עֶבֶד אָבִיךָ מֵאָז, וּמִקְרָא מְסֹרָס הוּא:


**Previously, I was your father's servant.** This is like [saying], Previously I was your father's servant. It is a verse that [must be] cut.¹⁵

footnotes:
¹⁵ The word וַאֲנִי needs to be “cut out” and placed in front of the phrase.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on II Samuel 15:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/15:37:1)


**יָבוֹא יְרוּשָׁלִָם.** נָתַן לִבּוֹ שֶׁיָּבֹא לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**Was going to come to Yerosholoyim.** Started thinking about coming to Yerusholoyim.¹⁶

footnotes:
¹⁶ יָבוֹא denotes he will come in the future.



## Chapter 16


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Samuel 16:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:1:1)


**וּמֵאָה קַיִץ.** (תרגום:) ״וּמְאָה מְנָן דְּבֵילְתָּא״:


**One hundred portions of dried figs.** Yonoson translates: "One hundred portions of dried figs."



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Samuel 16:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:10:1)


**כֹּה יְקַלֵּל.** כֵּן יְקַלֵּל:


**He should curse [me] like this.** He should curse like this.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 16:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:10:2)


**ה׳ אָמַר לוֹ.** אֶפְשָׁר אָדָם כְּמוֹתוֹ שֶׁהוּא רֹאשׁ לְסַנְהֶדְרִין, יְקַלֵּל אֶת הַמֶּלֶךְ אִם לֹא שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לוֹ מֵאֵת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**God told him.** Is it possible that a man like him who is head of the Sanhedrin would curse the king if he were not told [to do so] by God?



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Samuel 16:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:12:1)


**יִרְאֶה ה׳ בְּעֵינִי.** דִּמְעַת עֵינִי:


**God will see [the tears] in my eyes.** [Yonoson translates:] "The tears of my eyes."



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Samuel 16:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:14:1)


**וַיִּנָּפֵשׁ שָׁם.** בְּבַחוּרִים:


**And they rested there.** In Bachurim.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on II Samuel 16:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/16:21:1)


**וְחָזְקוּ יְדֵי כָּל אֲשֶׁר אִתָּךְ.** כִּי עַתָּה יָדָם רָפָה לַעֲזֹר לְךָ, שֶׁאוֹמְרִים בְּלִבָּם הַבֵּן יִתְחָרֵט אֵצֶל אָבִיו, וָאָנוּ נִהְיֶה שְׂנוּאִים לַמֶּלֶךְ:


**And you will strengthen the hands of all those who are with you.** Because at present their hands are feeble in their ability to help you because they are saying [to themselves] in their hearts, "The son will change towards [and reconcile with] his father and we will be hated by the king.¹

footnotes:
¹ For having joined a conspiracy against him.



## Chapter 17


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:3:1)


**כְּשׁוּב הַכֹּל.** אֵלֶיךָ:


**When they all return [to you].** To you.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:3:2)


**הָאִישׁ אֲשֶׁר אַתָּה מְבַקֵּשׁ.** יַעֲשֶׂה בּוֹ בַּקָּשָׁתְךָ וְיֵהָרֵג, וְאַחַר כָּךְ כָּל הָעָם יִהְיֶה שָׁלוֹם, וְזֶה מִקְרָא קָצָר:


**[Except] the man you want.** You will do what you want with him and you will have him killed. And afterwords, all the people will have peace. This is a shortened verse.¹

footnotes:
¹ Missing the words that Rashi adds here to make sense of the words.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:4:1)


**וּבְעֵינֵי זִקְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** זִקְנֵי אַשְׁמַאי:


**And in the eyes of all of the elders of Yisroel.** Wicked elders.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:8:1)


**וְאָבִיךָ אִישׁ מִלְחָמָה.** וְיוֹדֵעַ עִנְיְנֵי מִלְחָמָה וְטַכְסִיסֶיהָ וְטִיב מַאֲרָב, וּבָטוּחַ הוּא שֶׁתִּרְדֹּף הַלַּיְלָה לָבֹא עָלָיו, לְפִיכָךְ לֹא יָלִין עִם שְׁאָר הָעָם:


**Your father is a man of war.** He knows the concepts of war, its strategy and the nature of someone setting a trap. He is sure you will run after him tonight to get to him therefore he will not rest with the other people.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:9:1)


**הִנֵּה עַתָּה הוּא נֶחְבָּא.** וּכְשֶׁתָּבֹא עַל מַחֲנֵה הָעָם אֲשֶׁר אִתּוֹ, הוּא לֹא יִהְיֶה שָׁם שֶׁתַּהַרְגֶנּוּ:


**Now, he is hiding.** When you reach the encampment of the people with him, he will not be there for us to kill.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:9:2)


**וְהָיָה כִּנְפֹל בָּהֶם בַּתְּחִלָּה.** אֲנִי אָמַרְתִּי לְךָ כִּי אֲנָשָׁיו גִּבּוֹרִים וּמָרֵי נֶפֶשׁ, וְאִם יַהַרְגוּ בְּעַמְּךָ, תִּהְיֶה בָּם מַפָּלָה בְּמִלְחָמָה זוֹ שֶׁהִיא רִאשׁוֹנָה, וְשָׁמַע הַשּׁוֹמֵעַ מִכָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל הַבָּאִים לְהִתְחַבֵּר אֵלֶיךָ, וְאָמַר הָיְתָה מַגֵּפָה בָּעָם שֶׁל אַבְשָׁלוֹם:


**When some of the men fall at the beginning [of their battles].** I told you that his men are strong and embittered souls and if they kill some of your people that will [mean] a defeat in this battle which is the first. A person will hear, [i.e., a person] from among all of Yisroel who come to join you, and will say, There was a disaster amongst Avsholom's people'.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:10:1)


**וְהוּא גַם בֶּן חַיִל.** וְאוֹתוֹ הַשּׁוֹמֵעַ אֲפִלּוּ הוּא בֶּן חַיִל וְלִבּוֹ כְּלֵב הָאַרְיֵה, הִמֵּס יִמַּס בְּפַחַד וּרְעָדָה, וְלֹא יִתְקַשֵּׁר עוֹד אֵלֶיךָ, כִּי יֹאמַר כְּבָר הִתְחִיל לִפּוֹל וְנֶעֱנָשׁ הוּא עַל אָבִיו, וְלֹא יַצְלִיחַ:


**Even if he is a soldier.** That person who hears [about the disaster] even if he is a soldier and his heart is like that of a lion his heart will surely melt in fear and trepidation. And he will no longer join you because he will say, "He [Avsholom] has already started to fall and he is being punished for [what he did to] his father and he will not succeed.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:11:1)


**כִּי יָעַצְתִּי.** כִּי זֹאת עֲצָתִי:


**But I advise [as follows:]** Because this is my advice.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:11:2)


**וּפָנֶיךָ הוֹלְכִים בַּקְרָב.** בָּרֹאשׁ, (תרגום:) ״וְאַתְּ תְּהֵי אָזֵיל בְּרֵישׁ כּוּלָּנָא״:


**And you yourself should go to war.** At the head. [Yonoson translates:] "And then you should go at the head of everybody."



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:12:1)


**וְנַחְנוּ עָלָיו.** וְנָנוּחַ עָלָיו, לְשׁוֹן חֲנִיָּה, כְּמוֹ ״וַיָּנַח בְּכָל גְּבוּל מִצְרָיִם״ (שמות י:יד), ״נָחָה אֲרָם עַל אֶפְרָיִם״ (ישעיהו ז:ב):


**And we will encamp upon him.** We will encamp upon him language [denoting] camping as [we find], "It camped within the entire boorder of Mitzraim"² [and] "Arom has camped opposite Ephraim."³

footnotes:
² Shemos 10:14.
³ Yeshayohu 7:2.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:13:1)


**חֲבָלִים.** (תרגום:) ״מַשִׁרְיָן״, כְּמוֹ ״חֶבֶל נְבִיאִים״ (שמואל א י:ה) ״חֶבְלֵי רְשָׁעִים״ (תהלים קיט:סא):


**In groups.** [Yonoson translates:] "Groups" as [we find], "A group of prophets."⁴ [and] "Groups of evildoers."⁵

footnotes:
⁴ Shmuel I 10:5.
⁵ Tehilim 119:61.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:13:2)


**עַד הַנָּחַל.** חוֹמַת הָעִיר נִסְחוֹב אֶל הַגַּי:


**Into the valley [below].** We will drag the city wall into the valley.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:16:1)


**וְגַם עָבוֹר תַּעֲבֹר.** אֶת הַיַּרְדֵּן:


**Cross over it.** The Yardein.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:16:2)


**פֶּן יְבֻלַּע לַמֶּלֶךְ.** שֶׁלֹּא יָעַצְתִּיו לְטוֹבָה, וְיַעֲשֶׂה כְּדִבְרֵי אֲחִיתֹפֶל:


**Because [my suggestion] may be rejected by the king.** That I did not advise him well and [instead] he will proceed according to the advice of Achitophel.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:16:3)


**יְבֻלַּע.** יֵאָמֵר לוֹ בְּסֵתֶר וּבִבְלִיעָה:


**Rejected.** He will be told secretely and by a concealed method.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:17:1)


**בְּעֵין רֹגֵל.** (תרגום:) ״בְּעֵין קַצָּרָא״, כּוֹבְסֵי בִּגְדֵי צֶמֶר, שֶׁחוֹבְטִין אוֹתָם שָׁם, שֶׁבּוֹעֲטִים אוֹתָם בָּרֶגֶל:


**By the launderers' fountain.** [Yonason translates:] "By the launderers' fountain." Launderers of woolen garments that bring them [the garments] there by kicking them with [their] foot.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:19:1)


**אֶת הַמָּסָךְ.** וִילוֹן:


**The curtain.** Curtain.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:19:2)


**הָרִפוֹת.** חִטִּין כְּתוּשׁוֹת, כְּמוֹ ״בְּתוֹךְ הָרִיפוֹת״ (משלי כז:כב):


**Ground wheat.** Ground wheat as [we find], "Among the ground wheat."⁶

footnotes:
⁶ Mishlei 27:22.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:20:1)


**מִיכַל הַמָּיִם.** אֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ לוֹ פִּתְרוֹן בְּמַחְבֶּרֶת כַּל, וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ לְפִי עִנְיָנוֹ, כְּמוֹ שִׁבֹּלֶת הַנָּהָר:


**Stream of water.** I do not know the translation of this word מִיכַל. In the Machberes⁷ [under the entry] כַּל [he writes] that its translation is according to the context. [Here] its as [if to say] streaming water of the river.

footnotes:
⁷ Notebook written by Menachem (one of the Rishonim).



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:25:1)


**בַּת נָחָשׁ.** הוּא יִשַׁי אֲבִי דָוִד, רַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (בבא בתרא יז א): שֶׁמֵּת בְּלֹא עָוֹן, בְּעֶטְיוֹ שֶׁל נָחָשׁ:


**The daughter of Nochosh.** This is Yeshai, Dovid's father. Our Rabbis said that [Yeshai] died without sin [but only] because of the serpent's counsel.⁸

footnotes:
⁸ Baba Besra 16. The serpent in the Gan Fden who suggested to Chavah to eat from the Tree of knowledge. That sin brought death into the world. That is why Yeshai died because he had no sin of his own to merit death.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:27:1)


**וְשֹׁבִי בֶן נָחָשׁ.** הוּא חָנוּן בֶּן נָחָשׁ:


**Shovi, the son of Nochosh.** He is Chonun, the son of Nochosh.⁹

footnotes:
⁹ Mentioned earlier in 10:1.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:28:1)


**מִשְׁכָּב.** מַצָּעוֹת:


**Mats.** Mats.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:28:2)


**וְסַפּוֹת.** כְּלֵי תַשְׁמִישׁ הֵם, כְּמוֹ ״סִפּוֹת כֶּסֶף״ (מלכים ב יב:יד):


**Utensils.** They are utensils for practical use. as [we find], "Silver utensils."¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ Melochim II, 12:14.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:28:3)


**וְקָלִי.** מִינֵי קְלָיוֹת, אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (עבודה זרה לח ב): שְׁנֵי מִינֵי שְׁתִיתָא הִקְרִיב בַּרְזִלַּי לְדָוִד, אֶחָד שֶׁל חִטִּים, וְאֶחָד שֶׁל עֲדָשִׁים, כְּעִנְיָן הָאָמוּר כָּאן; **קֶמַח וְקָלִי**, הוּא שֶׁל דָּגָן, **וּפוֹל וַעֲדָשִׁים וְקָלִי**, מִמִּינִים שֶׁל קִטְנִיּוֹת שֶׁמְּיַבְּשִׁין אוֹתָן בַּתַּנּוּר כְּשֶׁהֵן לַחִין, וְהֵם מְתוּקִין לְעוֹלָם, וְטוֹחֲנִין אוֹתָן, וְעוֹשִׂין מֵהֶם מַאֲכָל שֶׁקּוֹרִין שְׁתִיתָא:


**Toasted grain.** Types of toasted foods Our Rabbis said Barzilai brought two types of porridge to Dovid, one made of wheat and one made of lentils as described here flour and קָלִי, this one was made from wheat. Beans, lentils and קָלִי [this one] was made from types of beans.¹¹ They used to dry them in ovens [having been put in] while they were [still] moist, they [thus] were permenantly sweet [then] they ground them and made from them a food that was called "porridge."

footnotes:
¹¹ The word קָלִי appears twice in this verse. The first קָלִי written after קֶמַח refers to a porridge made from grain. The second קָלִי written after עֲדָשִׁים refers to a porridge made from lentils.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on II Samuel 17:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/17:29:1)


**וּשְׁפוֹת בָּקָר.** (תרגום:) ״וְגוֹבְנִין דְּחָלָב״:


**And cow's cheese.** [Yonoson translates:] "And cheese made from milk."



## Chapter 18


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:3:1)


**לֹא יָשִׂימוּ אֵלֵינוּ לֵב.** לֹא יִהְיֶה דָבָר חָשׁוּב בְּעֵינֵיהֶם לְהִתְגַּבֵּר וּלְהִתְהַלֵּל, מֵאַחַר שֶׁאֵין אַתָּה בַּמִּלְחָמָה:


**They will not pay us any attention.** It will not be important in their eyes to overpower and to boast [[victoriously] now that you are not in the war.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:3:2)


**כִּי עַתָּה.** בִּהְיוֹתְךָ בִּכְבוֹדְךָ, חָשׁוּב אַתָּה כָּמוֹנוּ:


**Because now.** While you are in your glory [uncaptured] you are as important as all of us [together].


###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:3:3)


**עֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים.** כַּעֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים אִישׁ כָּמוֹנוּ:


**Ten thousand.** Like us ten thousand men.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:3:4)


**כִּי תִהְיֶה לָּנוּ מֵעִיר לַעֲזוֹר.** (תרגום:) ״וּכְעַן טָב אֲרֵי תְצַלֵּי עֲלָנָא מִן קַרְתָּא לְמִסְעַד״:


**If you help us from the city.** [Yonoson translates:] "It will be better if you pray for us from the city for help.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:5:1)


**לְאַט לִי לַנַּעַר.** אִם יִקָּרֵא לִפְנֵיכֶם בַּמִּלְחָמָה, חִמְלוּ עָלָיו:


**Be gentle to the young man.** If he presents himself before you in battle have pity on him.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:6:1)


**בְּיַעַר אֶפְרָיִם.** וּמֵהֵיכָן הָיָה יַעַר לְאֶפְרַיִם בְּעֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן מִזְרָחָה, שֶׁלֹּא נִתַּן שָׁם חֵלֶק אֶלָּא לִבְנֵי גָד וְלִבְנֵי רְאוּבֵן וְלִמְנַשֶּׁה, אֶלָּא מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהִתְנָה יְהוֹשֻׁעַ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מַרְעִין בְּחוֹרָשִׁין, וְהָיָה אוֹתוֹ הַיַּעַר סָמוּךְ לְאֶפְרַיִם, אֶלָּא שֶׁהַיַּרְדֵּן מַפְסִיק, וְהָיוּ מַרְעִין שָׁם בְּהֵמוֹתֵיהֶם, הָיָה נִקְרָא יַעַר אֶפְרָיִם:


**In the forest of Ephraim.** How did Ephraim get possession of a forest on the eastern side of the Yardein,¹ no one had a portion there except for the people of Gad, Reuvein and Menashe? Yehoshua [partitioned Israel] conditionally that all the tribes could [have their cattle] graze in any forest. This forest was near Ephraim's portion² except that the Yardein was in between, and the people of Ephraim had their cattle graze there. [Thus] it was called Ephraim's forest

footnotes:
¹ Dovid had crossed over to the eastern side of the Yardein earlier (17:22) and that’s where this battle took place.
² On the other side of the Yardein.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:8:1)


**וַיֶּרֶב הַיַּעַר לֶאֱכֹל בָּעָם.** חַיּוֹת רָעוֹת שֶׁבַּיַּעַר, כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**The [wild beasts of the] forest killed more people.** The wild beasts of the forest, so Yonason translated.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:9:1)


**וַיִּקָּרֵא אַבְשָׁלוֹם.** לְשׁוֹן מִקְרֶה וּפֶגַע:


**Avsholom happenned.** Denoting something that just happened, a chance meeting.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:9:2)


**וַיֻּתַּן בֵּין הַשָּׁמַיִם וּבֵין הָאָרֶץ.** אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (סוטה י ב): שָׁלַף חַרְבּוֹ לָקוּץ שְׂעָרוֹ, וְרָאָה גֵּיהִנֹּם פְּתוּחָה תַּחְתָּיו:


**He was hanging between the sky and the earth.** Our Rabbis said³ He unsheathed his sword to cut his hair and saw Gehennom open beneath him.

footnotes:
³ Sotah 10b. See Meharsho there.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:12:1)


**שִׁמְרוּ מִי בַנַּעַר.** כָּל מִי שֶׁיָּבֹא לְיָדוֹ יִשְׁמְרֶנּוּ:


**Beware that none harm the young man.** Everyone who meets up with him should preserve him.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:14:1)


**לֹא כֵן אוֹחִילָה לְפָנֶיךָ.** לֹא אֲבַקֵּשׁ עוֹד בַּקָּשָׁה מִמְּךָ, כִּי אֲנִי אֵלֵךְ:


**"I will not ask you [any further]."** I will not make any more requests of you because I will go myself.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:18:1)


**כִּי אָמַר אֵין לִי בֵן.** מְמַלֵּא מְקוֹמִי, וְיִהְיֶה חָשׁוּב כְּמוֹתִי:


**Because he said, "I have no son."** That can replace me and be as important as me.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ He did have children as related earlier (14:27) but did not consider them on a par with his stature. See Sotah 11a.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:18:2)


**בַּעֲבוּר הַזְכִּיר שְׁמִי.** אָקִים לִי מַצֶּבֶת אֶבֶן, וּבִנְיָן חָשׁוּב הָיָה:


**This will memorialize my name.** I will build this stone monument for myself. It was a significant building.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:20:1)


**וּבִשַּׂרְתָּ בְּיוֹם אַחֵר.** בְּשׂוֹרַת תְּשׁוּעָה אַחֶרֶת:


**Inform him another day.** A tiding of some other victory.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:20:2)


**כִּי עַל כֵּן.** כִּי עַל אֲשֶׁר בֶּן הַמֶּלֶךְ מֵת, לְפִיכָךְ לֹא טוֹבָה לְךָ הַבְּשׂוֹרָה זֹאת:


**Because.** As a result of the king's son's death therefor this announcement will not be good for you.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:22:1)


**וּלְכָה אֵין בְּשׂוֹרָה מוֹצֵאת.** אֵין מַתַּת שְׂכַר בְּשׂוֹרָה מְצוּיָה הַיּוֹם:


**For you. there is no reward in this announcement.** There is no reward available today for an announcement.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:23:1)


**וִיהִי מָה.** כְּלוֹמַר וּמָה בְּכָךְ אִם אֵין לִי שָׂכָר:


**So what.** In other words, so what if I get no reward?


###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:23:2)


**וַיַּעֲבוֹר.** (תרגום:) ״וְקָדִים יָת כּוּשִׁי״:


**And he passed.** [Yonoson translates:] He arrived before the Kushite.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:29:1)


**רָאִיתִי הֶהָמוֹן הַגָּדוֹל.** הוֹמִים וְנָעִים בַּמִּלְחָמָה:


**I saw a great multitude [engaged in battle].** Moving to and fro in battle.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:29:2)


**לִשְׁלוֹחַ אֶת עֶבֶד הַמֶּלֶךְ יוֹאָב וְאֶת עַבְדֶּךָ.** הֲרֵי זֶה מִקְרָא מְסוֹרָס, לִשְׁלוֹחַ יוֹאָב אֶת עֶבֶד הַמֶּלֶךְ וְאֶת עַבְדֶּךָ, לְאַחַר שֶׁשָּׁלַח יוֹאָב אֶת עֶבֶד הַמֶּלֶךְ זֶה הָרָץ אַחֲרֵי:


**And Yoav sent one of the king's other servants and [me] your servant.** This is a verse that needs to be rearranged as follows: Yoav sent one of the king's other servants and [me] your servant. After Yoav sent the king's other servant, [referring to] the one who is running behind me.⁵

footnotes:
⁵ After he sent that runner is when he sent me.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:29:3)


**וְאֶת עַבְדֶּךָ.** עַל עַצְמוֹ הוּא אוֹמֵר, וְלֹא יָדַעְתִּי מָה הָיָה אַחֲרֵי כֵן:


**And [me] your servant.** He is referring to himself. I do not know what happenned afterwords.⁶

footnotes:
⁶ Here Rashi is explaining the last part of the verse, “and I do not know what…”



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on II Samuel 18:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/18:30:1)


**סֹב הִתְיַצֵּב כֹּה.** פְּנֵה לְצַד אַחֵר וְהִתְיַצֵּב כֹּה, וְנִשְׁמַע מַה יֹּאמַר זֶה:


**Turn and stand there.** Move to the other side and stand there and let us hear what this one [the kushite] says.



## Chapter 19


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:1:1)


**בְּנִי בְנִי.** שְׁמוֹנֶה פְּעָמִים, אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (סוטה י ב): שִׁבְעָה דְּאַסְקֵיהּ מִשִּׁבְעָה מְדוֹרֵי גֵּיהִנֹּם, וְחַד דְּאַיְיתֵיהּ לְעָלְמָא דְּאָתֵי:


**My son, my son.** Eight times.¹ Our Rabbis said,² Seven of those times to lift him out of the seven levels of Gehennom and one to to bring him into the World to Come.

footnotes:
¹ Dovid said the word בְּנִי  eight times, five times in this verse and another three times later in verse 5.
² Sotah 10b.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:5:1)


**לָאַט אֶת פָּנָיו.** (תרגום:) ״כְּרִיךְ יָת אַפּוֹהִי״, כְּמִשְׁפָּט הָאֲבֵלִים:


**Covered his face.** [Yononson translates:] "Covered his face" in the manner of mourners.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:5:2)


**לָאַט.** כְּמוֹ ״וַיָּלֶט פָּנָיו בְּאַדַּרְתּוֹ״ (מלכים א יט:יג); ״הִנֵּה הִיא לוּטָה בַשִּׂמְלָה״ (שמואל א כא:י):


**Covered.** As [we find]: "He wrapped his face with his coat",³ "It is wrapped up in a cloth".⁴

footnotes:
³ Melochim I, 19:13.
⁴ Shmuel I, 21:10.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:8:1)


**כִּי אֵינְךָ יוֹצֵא.** אִם אֵינְךָ יוֹצֵא:


**That [if] you do not go out [to the people].** If you do not go out.⁵

footnotes:
⁵ Rashi is adding the word “if” to the verse.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:9:1)


**וְיִשְׂרָאֵל נָס.** אוֹתָם שֶׁהָיוּ עִם אַבְשָׁלוֹם:


**[As the people of] Yisroel fled.** Those people that were with Avsholom.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:10:1)


**כָּל הָעָם נָדוֹן.** מִתְוַכְּחִים זֶה עִם זֶה:


**The people were reproaching each other.** Reproaching each other.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:12:1)


**וּדְבַר כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל בָּא אֶל הַמֶּלֶךְ.** כָּל זֶה מִדִּבְרֵי שְׁלִיחוּתוֹ:


**While the messages of all Yisroel are coming to the king.** All this was part of the message he was charged to bring [to the elders].



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:14:1)


**וְלַעֲמָשָׂא.** שֶׁהוּא שַׂר צָבָא, תֹּאמְרוּ; הֲלֹא בֶּן אֲחוֹתִי אַתָּה:


**To Amosoh.** Who is [Avsholom's] commanding officer you should say, 'You are my own sister's son.'⁶

footnotes:
⁶ The son of Avigail. See 17:25.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:18:1)


**וְצָלְחוּ הַיַּרְדֵּן.** (תרגום:) ״בְּקָעוּהוּ בִּתְרִיסֵיהוֹן״:


**They crossed the Yardein.** They crossed it with their rafts.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:19:1)


**וְעָבְרָה הָעֲבָרָה.** סְפִינַת הַמַּעְבָּרָה שֶׁעוֹבְרִים בָּהּ אֶת עֵבֶר רֹחַב הַנַּחַל:


**The ferry crossed over.** The ferry boat that was used to cross over to the other side of the river's width.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:21:1)


**רִאשׁוֹן לְכָל בֵּית יוֹסֵף.** כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל חָטְאוּ לְךָ, וַאֲנִי יוֹתֵר מִכֻּלָּם, וְהִנְנִי בָּאתִי לְבַקֵּשׁ מְחִילָה, אִם תְּקַבְּלֵנִי, בְּטוּחִים כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁתְּקַבְּלֵם, וְאִם לָאו, יִהְיוּ יְרֵאִים לָשׁוּב אֵלֶיךָ עוֹד:


**First from among all the House of Yoseif.** All of Yisroel wronged you, I more then everyone. I have come to ask you forgiveness, if you accept me then all of Yisroel will be confident that you will accept them but if you do not [accept me] they will be afraid to return to you.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:23:1)


**הַיּוֹם יוּמַת אִישׁ.** בִּתְמִיהָ, כִּי הַיּוֹם אֲנִי מֶלֶךְ, עַד עַכְשָׁיו הָיִיתִי סָבוּר, לֹא זִלְזֵל בִּי אָדָם גָּדוֹל כָּזֶה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן נִפְסְקָה מַלְכוּתִי מֵאֵת הַמָּקוֹם וְהוּא אָמַר לוֹ קַלֵּל אֶת דָּוִד, אֲבָל עַכְשָׁיו שֶׁהוּא מִתְחָרֵט, יָדַעְתִּי כִּי מֶלֶךְ אֲנִי:


**Should a man die today?** This is a question. Because today I am [truly] king. Until now I thought A great man as this [Shimi] would not have shamed me unless God had decreed to end my kingdom, and He had told him, 'Curse Dovid.' But now that he regrets it I know that I am [truly] king.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:25:1)


**וְלֹא עָשָׂה רַגְלָיו.** לְשׁוֹן תִּקּוּן הוּא, הַעֲבָרַת שֵׂעָר שֶׁבֵּין הָרַגְלַיִם:


**He had not tended to the hair [between] his legs.** Denoting fixing up, the removing of the hair between his legs.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:25:2)


**שְׂפָמוֹ.** גרנו״ן בלע״ז:


**His moustache.** *Grenon* in O.F.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:26:1)


**לָמָּה לֹא הָלַכְתָּ עִמִּי.** לָצֵאת מִירוּשָׁלַיִם כְּשֶׁיָּצָאתִי:


**Why did you not you go with me?** To leave Yerusholoyim when I left.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:27:1)


**כִּי פִסֵּחַ עַבְדֶּךָ.** וְאֵינִי יָכוֹל לֵילֵךְ בְּרַגְלִי:


**Because your servant is lame.** And I can not walk on foot.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:32:1)


**לְשַׁלְּחוֹ.** לְלַוּוֹתוֹ:


**Accompanying him.** To accompany him.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:33:1)


**כִּי אִישׁ גָּדוֹל הוּא.** בְּעֹשֶׁר:


**Because he was a very great man.** In terms of wealth.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Samuel/r/19:35:1)


**כַּמָּה יְמֵי שְׁנֵי חַיַּי.** אֲשֶׁר יֵשׁ לִי לִחְיוֹת:


**How many more years of life [do I have].** That I have to live.


###### Rashi on II Samuel 19:35:2
